# KTM 150 SX 2020: aftermarket parts

4996 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 450 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1918 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2628 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
